Declining an invitation: If someone invites you to an event or activity that doesn't align with your interests or values, you might say, \"Thanks for the invite, but I don't roll like that.\" This could mean you're not into that type of scene, or you prefer to spend your time differently.\n\n2. Rejecting a proposal or suggestion: If someone suggests an unethical or inappropriate course of action, you could respond with, \"I don't roll like that,\" indicating that you don't engage in such behavior.\n\n3. For example, if someone assumes you'd like a certain type of music or fashion, you might say, \"Nah, I don't roll like that.
